### [79] [Using Codebooks to Detect Cybercrime Topics in Text Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16000)
*Shufan Chai,Liangliang Sun,Jessica Staddon*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于LLM提示的方法，利用定性网络犯罪研究中的codebooks来检测消费者叙述中的网络犯罪主题，在冒充诈骗和身份盗窃两类任务上验证了Gemini和GPT系列5个模型的高精确率与召回率，为资源受限的地方政府提供可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国联邦机构人员削减，网络犯罪相关消费者投诉的管理越来越多地落在州和城市政府身上。LLM在文本投诉的网络犯罪检测上具有潜力，但通常依赖地方政府无力开发和维护的专用模型。

Method: 提出一种LLM提示方法，将定性网络犯罪研究中的codebooks用于提示，以检测消费者叙述中的网络犯罪主题；在冒充诈骗和身份盗窃两个主题上进行评估。

Result: 在Gemini和GPT两大模型家族的5个模型多次运行中，该方法均实现了高精确率和高召回率。

Conclusion: 该策略为资源受限的组织（如许多地方政府）利用前沿模型支持社区安全提供了可行路径。

Abstract: In the United States, management of cybercrime-related consumer complaints increasingly falls on state and city governments given de-staffing of federal agencies. AI, and in particular, large language models (LLMs), shows promise for detecting cybercrime in text complaints, but often via specialized models that local governments are not resourced to develop and maintain. We present an LLM prompting method that uses codebooks from qualitative cybercrime research to detect cybercrime topics in consumer narratives. For two cybercrime topics, impostor scams and identity theft, we demonstrate the method achieves high precision and recall across multiple runs of 5 models in the Gemini and GPT model families. This strategy suggests a path for resource-constrained organizations, like many local governments, to leverage frontier models to support community safety.

</details>


### [80] [Measuring AI harms with multidimensional Lorenz Zonoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16004)
*Paolo Giudici,Jose' Maria Sarabia,Sofia Vei*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该论文提出用Lorenz Zonoids和Gini指数（扩展至多维）来建模AI危害数据，以严重性而非仅可能性为基础进行风险评估，并基于MIT的真实AI事故数据实证分析，发现环境、基础设施、财产、物理和民主相关危害的集中度最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统日益影响高风险社会领域，但现有风险管理方法缺乏基于真实危害（考虑严重性而非仅可能性）的方法，导致治理模型以合规和提供者为中心，无法揭示危害的危险性及干预优先次序。危害数据通常是有序且多维的，进一步加剧了这一问题。

Method: 将危害数据用Lorenz Zonoids和Gini指数建模，并将其扩展至多维设定，然后在MIT提供的真实AI事故数据仓库上实际计算这些指标。

Result: 环境、基础设施、财产、物理和民主相关危害在两个多维Gini指数下取值最高，表现出在其直接的、间接的和推断的严重性-频率联合分布中最强的集中度。

Conclusion: 这些集中度模式有助于在确定缓减优先事项时识别需要更仔细审查的危害类别，为AI风险治理提供更有效的评估方法。

Abstract: While AI systems increasingly shape high-stakes societal domains, their governance is limited by the lack of risk management methods that operate on real harms, taking their severity, and not only their likelihood, into account. As a consequence, AI risk management models remain compliance-driven and provider-centric, offering limited insight into how harms are dangerous, and on what should be the priority of intervention. The problem is amplified by the nature of harm data which are typically ordinal and multidimensional. To solve the problem, and offer an effective risk assessment methodology, in this paper we propose to model harm data by means of Lorenz Zonoids and Gini indices. To this aim we propose to extend them in a multidimensional setting, and show how to practically calculate them for a real AI incident data repository, provided by the Massachusetts Institute of Technology. The empirical findings indicate that environmental, infrastructure, property, physical, and democracy-related harms attain the highest values under the two multidimensional Gini indices and therefore exhibit the strongest concentration in their joint direct, indirect, and inferred severity-frequency distributions. These concentration patterns may help identify categories that warrant closer examination when mitigation priorities are determined.

</details>


### [81] [Beyond Cultural Knowledge: Evaluating Arabic Cultural Appropriateness of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16006)
*Enes Altinisik,Hamdy Mubarak,Masoomali Fatehkia,Husrev_Taha_Sencar Husrev Taha Sencar*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文提出 AraBehave 基准（1,623 条阿拉伯语开放式文化相关提示、29,214 条母语者文化得体性判断及评分模型），发现文化得体性由"规范立场"和"文化事实准确性"两个近乎独立的成分构成；通用模型与阿拉伯语专用模型总分相当但失败原因迥异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文化评测多考察模型"知道什么"，而非在开放式推荐、观点和指导中"如何表现"，且缺少阿拉伯地区母语者视角的开放式评测。

Method: 构建 AraBehave：1,623 条文化相关的开放式阿拉伯语提示 + 29,214 条来自多个阿拉伯地区母语者的文化得体性判断，并训练与人类判断强相关（Pearson r=0.74）的评分模型；评测 3 个阿拉伯语中心模型与 3 个前沿 LLM，并做指令扰动实验。

Result: 文化得体性分解为规范立场与接地文化准确性；最佳通用与最佳阿拉伯语中心模型同分（3.84 vs 3.83）但失败原因不同——通用模型事实接地强却立场不当（28–33% 低分理由为世俗框架、虚假平衡），阿拉伯语模型立场正确却因伪造圣训、误引经文被扣分（29%）。一句文化指令即可把 Gemini 提升至 4.57；中性"客观"提示使 Allam-7B 降 0.68 分；改用英语提问几乎使所有模型降分。通用安全基准饱和（>89），无法捕捉上述差异。

Conclusion: 文化得体性是多维、可分离的能力，立场易通过少量指令改善但脆弱，接地性依赖规模与阿拉伯语对齐数据；现有安全基准无法反映文化得体性，作者将发布基准、标注与评分模型。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly serve users whose expectations are shaped by their cultural context, yet most cultural evaluations test what a model knows rather than how it behaves when giving open-ended recommendations, opinions, and guidance. We introduce AraBehave: 1,623 culturally grounded, open-ended Arabic prompts with 29,214 cultural-appropriateness judgments from native speakers across several Arab regions, plus a scoring model whose predictions correlate strongly with human judgments on unseen systems (Pearson r=0.74). Evaluating three Arabic-centric and three frontier LLMs, we find that cultural appropriateness is not a single capability but decomposes into two largely independent components: normative stance and grounded cultural accuracy. The best general-purpose and best Arabic-centric models score identically (3.84 vs. 3.83 of 5) yet almost never fail for the same reason: general-purpose models exhibit strong factual grounding but a culturally inappropriate normative stance, being penalized for secular framing and false balance on culturally settled matters (28--33% of their low-score rationales), while the best Arabic-centric model adopts the expected stance but is penalized for fabricated hadith and misquoted verses (29%). Stance is cheap and fragile: one sentence of cultural instruction lifts Gemini to 4.57, above every Arabic-specialized model. Conversely, a generic ``answer clearly and objectively'' prompt costs Allam-7B 0.68 points, while asking the same questions in English lowers scores for every model but one. Grounding instead tracks scale and Arabic alignment data, and disappears when culturally aware instruction tuning is replaced by a culture-neutral corpus. General safety benchmarks see none of this: they saturate above 89 while cultural scores span 2.71-3.84. We will release the benchmark, annotations, and the scoring model.

</details>


### [82] [AI-Driven Feedback Systems, Digital Labour, and Silent Quitting: Transforming African Workplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16192)
*Abayomi O. Agbeyangi,Jose M. Lukose*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨AI驱动的反馈机制如何影响非洲职场中的"隐性离职"现象，并提出以非洲为中心的负责任技术应用建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲组织日益采用远程/混合办公和数字化HR管理，员工"隐性离职"（人在岗但缺乏投入与情感）问题凸显。现有关于数字劳动与自动化的讨论缺乏非洲视角，需研究AI反馈机制在识别和应对员工脱岗中的作用与风险。

Method: 概念性/理论分析。考察AI驱动的反馈机制（情感分析、脉动调查、聊天机器人、参与度仪表盘、预测分析）在非洲公私部门中实现持续倾听、即时绩效反馈和主动员工沟通的方式，并结合数字不平等、基础设施不足、隐私、算法偏见和职场监控等挑战进行分析。

Result: AI能够帮助组织早期识别员工脱岗，支持及时干预与改善沟通；但在发展中国家应用面临数字不平等、基础设施欠缺、隐私侵蚀、算法偏见及监控风险等社会经济与治理挑战。

Conclusion: 研究将"隐性离职"置于数字劳动与自动化的大讨论中，贡献了非洲中心视角，并为HR从业者、管理者、政策制定者和技术开发者提供了负责任、情境敏感的职场转型建议。

Abstract: The current trend of digitalisation has revolutionised the organisation of work and the way it is measured and performed across the globe, with AI becoming more common for managing labour and performance, as well as employee communication. In African organisations, where there is increasing adoption of remote work, hybrid models of work, digital collaboration, and data-based HR management, the notion of silent quitting has become more relevant, defined as worker disengagement when employees are still doing their job but do not put any effort into achieving good performance and exhibiting any emotion. This paper investigates how AI-driven feedback mechanisms, including sentiment analysis systems, pulse surveys, chatbots, engagement dashboards, and predictive analytics, are changing African workplaces through offering continuous listening, instant performance information and proactive engagement with employees. The study also explores how AI can assist organisations in identifying early disengagement and enable intervention and better employee communication in both private and public sector organisations in Africa. At the same time, we address the challenges of socioeconomic development and governance posed by AI implementation in developing countries, including digital inequality, infrastructure shortcomings, privacy concerns, algorithmic bias, and the risk of workplace surveillance. By situating silent quitting within wider debates on digital labour and automation, the paper contributes an African-centred perspective to discussions on the future of work and offers practical recommendations for HR professionals, managers, policymakers, and technology developers seeking responsible, context-sensitive approaches to workplace transformation across the continent.

</details>


### [83] [Mapping U.S. Federal AI Governance Against Sector Vulnerability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16260)
*Ho Ting Hung,Angelica Chowdhury,James Teague,Simon Mylius,Spencer Michaels,Peter Slattery,Alexander Saeri,Neil Thompson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文评估684份美国联邦AI治理文件对14个行业和24类AI风险的覆盖广度与深度，并与272位专家的脆弱性评估对比，发现治理覆盖与专家评估的风险水平存在明显错配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在不同行业带来不同程度的风险，但美国联邦AI治理是否反映了这些差异尚不清楚，需系统评估覆盖情况并识别治理缺口。

Method: 对684份联邦AI治理文件进行编码，衡量14个行业与24类AI风险的覆盖广度（出现频率）和深度（讨论实质程度），再将各风险的行业覆盖模式与272位专家Delphi研究的脆弱性评估进行对比。

Result: 覆盖差异显著：鲁棒性、系统安全和治理相关风险受关注较多，而社会经济、环境及新兴风险（含多智能体风险）较少；公共管理、国家安全、信息和科学服务覆盖较高，而金融、医疗等专家认为高度脆弱的行业覆盖较低。

Conclusion: 通过映射当前覆盖并识别其与专家脆弱性评估的差距，揭示了潜在的AI治理缺口，可为政府和行业的AI风险决策提供参考。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) poses different levels of risk across sectors, but are these differences reflected in U.S. federal AI governance? To help answer this question, we assess 684 federal AI governance documents for their coverage of 14 sectors and 24 AI risks. We measure coverage as breadth (i.e., how frequently the risk or sector is addressed across documents) and depth (i.e., how substantively the risk or sector is discussed). We then compare sector coverage patterns for each of the 24 risks with vulnerability assessments from a Delphi study of 272 experts. Our analysis finds substantial variation in coverage: AI risks related to robustness, system security, and governance receive more attention than socioeconomic, environmental, and emerging risks, including multi-agent risks. Public administration, national security, information, and scientific services receive comparatively high levels of coverage relative to other sectors, such as finance and healthcare, which experts rate as highly vulnerable to AI risks. By mapping current coverage and identifying where it differs from expert assessments of vulnerability, we surface potential AI governance gaps which may help inform AI risk-related decisions across government and industry.

</details>


### [84] [Do job seekers value procedure in AI hiring only for error correction? Evidence from a conjoint experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16390)
*Chuyao Wang,Patrick Sturgis,Daniel de Kadt*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 1,919名美国求职者参与的预注册配对联合实验表明，程序本身的价值并非仅来自纠错，人力参与比任何程序特征都重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雇主越来越多地将初筛交给自动系统，申请人在无人阅读前就可能被拒。接受度可能取决于系统表现和决策程序两方面，但以往研究很少独立变化程序与表现，因此不清楚申请人看重程序是出于其本身价值，还是为了纠错。

Method: 预注册配对联合实验，1,919名美国求职者在决策权、错误率、解释、退出、申诉和独立偏见审计六个独立随机化属性之间做出八次选择。

Result: 申诉、退出和偏见审计的价值并不随错误拒绝增多而上升，均在预注册等价界内。人力参与权重高于任何程序特征，其影响相当于把错误拒绝率从30%降至10%。

Conclusion: 结果限制了简单的纠错解释，符合申请人部分出于程序本身价值而重视程序，因此提升系统表现不能替代申请人可援引的权利。

Abstract: Employers increasingly delegate initial screening to automated systems, which in many cases reject an application before any human reads it. Acceptance of such systems plausibly depends both on how well they perform and on the procedure that produces the decision. Prior studies rarely vary procedure and performance independently, leaving it unclear whether applicants value procedure for its own sake or for the errors it corrects. In a preregistered paired-profile conjoint experiment, 1,919 United States job seekers made eight choices between systems with independently randomized levels of decision authority, error rate, explanation, opt-out, appeal, and independent bias audit. The value of the appeal, the opt-out, and the bias audit did not rise as wrongful rejections became more common, each staying within a preregistered equivalence bound. Human involvement carried more weight than any procedural feature, moving stated choice about as much as cutting wrongful rejections from 30% to 10%. These patterns constrain a simple error-correction account and are consistent with applicants valuing procedure partly for its own sake, so that improving a system's performance does not substitute for a right applicants can invoke.

</details>


### [85] [Silicon sampling answers with country-level assumptions, not individual attitudes: Cross-national evidence from the European Social Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16395)
*Chuyao Wang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究用欧洲社会调查第11轮（30国42项）评估LLM"硅采样"能否再现跨国差异，发现聚合层面恢复中等且不均衡，加入国名能将中位相关从-0.03提至0.52，但个体层面恢复可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅采样（用LLM模拟受访者）能否复现跨国差异及其原因尚不清楚，需系统检验其有效性边界。

Method: 以ESS第11轮（30国、42项题目）为基准，用两个开源LLM，在第一人称与第三人称提示下测试，并做背景故事（人口统计画像）与作答格式实验；与"邻国平均"这一无LLM基线比较。

Result: 聚合恢复中等且逐题不均；在三变量人口背景中加入国名使模拟与观测国均值的中位相关由-0.03升至0.52，更丰富画像无稳定增益；国名标签起国家级假设作用，细节无法改变；用文字标注量表端点可纠正国家排序倒置，即作答格式决定排序方向；个体层面恢复在各条件下均近于零，且与聚合恢复不相关；邻国平均基线在国别水平上比所有模型条件更准、排序也相当。

Conclusion: 硅采样在逐题验证并报告作答格式的前提下，可用于探索性国别排序比较；不支持个体或分布层面的推断。

Abstract: Silicon sampling uses large language models (LLMs) to simulate survey respondents. Whether it recovers cross-national variation, and why, remains unresolved. This study evaluates it against European Social Survey Round 11 (30 countries, 42 items) with two open-weight LLMs under first- and third-person prompts, plus backstory and response-format experiments. Aggregate recovery is moderate and uneven across items. Adding the country name to a three-variable demographic backstory raises the median per-item correlation between simulated and observed country means from -0.03 to 0.52, and the richer profiles tested add no consistent gain. The respondent's country label acts as a country-level assumption that respondent detail does not revise. Naming the response-scale endpoints in words stops the model from ranking countries backwards, so the answer format sets the direction of the ranking. Individual-level recovery remains negligible in every condition and does not track aggregate recovery across countries. An average of neighboring countries, using no LLM, recovers country levels more accurately than every model condition and ranks them about as well. Silicon sampling can thus support exploratory country-ranking comparison after item-level validation and with the response format reported. It does not support individual or distributional inference.

</details>


### [86] [Dataset repurposing and disruptive AI research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16736)
*Yulin Yu,Yong-Yeol Ahn,Daniel M. Romero*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文基于"重组式新颖性"与"转化式创造力"两大理论框架，分析逾1万篇机器学习论文，探讨数据再利用的科学影响。发现数据再利用与更高颠覆性相关，被后续研究采用时影响更大，且再利用团队往往更具经验、声望及产学合作背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据需求激增、高质量数据集创建困难、易获取数据源枯竭，促使研究者思考如何通过数据的重组与再利用来最大化现有数据集的价值。

Method: 结合"重组式新颖性"和"转化式创造力"两个理论框架，对超过1万篇机器学习论文的科学成果进行实证分析，考察数据再利用行为及其科学影响。

Result: 1) 多数被再利用数据集短期内未获广泛关注，但数据再利用与更高颠覆性相关;2) 当再利用数据被后续研究采用时，该论文具有更高颠覆性与引用影响;3) 再利用团队经验更丰富、机构声望更高、更多产学合作，但团队特征难以预测哪些数据集会被社区采纳。

Conclusion: 数据再利用可能是科学发现的重要途径，其成功被采纳在更大团队及产学合作中更为常见。

Abstract: Technological advancements are enabling increasingly systematic and large-scale data collection across all areas of science, driving scientific innovation. In particular, AI research exemplifies this trend, having advanced rapidly through the assembly of massive datasets used to train and evaluate machine learning models. However, the escalating demand for data, the difficulty of creating high-quality datasets, and the exhaustion of easily accessible data sources in AI research raise important questions about how to maximize the value of existing datasets through recombination and repurposing. Here, we draw on two theoretical frameworks---recombinational novelty and transformational creativity---to examine the practice of data repurposing and its scientific impact. Focusing on AI, we analyze scientific outcomes associated with data repurposing across more than 10,000 machine learning papers. First, we find that although most repurposed datasets do not achieve broad visibility in the short term, data repurposing is associated with greater disruption. Second, when repurposed data is adopted by subsequent research, the repurposing paper is associated with higher disruption and increased citation impact. Third, repurposing teams tend to be more experienced, more institutionally prestigious, and involve academic--industry collaboration. However, team characteristics poorly predict which repurposed datasets will be adopted by the community. These findings suggest that data repurposing may be an important approach to scientific discovery, and that its successful adoption is more common among larger teams and collaborations spanning academia and industry.

</details>


### [87] [AI literacy over tool design: a mixed-methods study of scaffolded versus unrestricted generative AI in programming education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16784)
*Sepinoud Azimi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 一项为期七周的混合方法试点研究在数据分析硕士课程中随机比较了"嵌入式AI学习教练"与"自由使用AI工具"两种方案，发现工具设计对学习成绩影响有限，真正决定表现的是学生自我管理AI使用的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI已成为编程教育的常规资源，而大多数院校的应对方式偏向"控制"——限制访问或提供受控版本。作者希望检验一种基于脚手架理论的引导式方案是否比自由使用更能提升学习效果、增强信心并减少过度依赖。

Method: 在硕士级数据分析课程中开展七周混合方法试点，33名学生随机分为两组：一组使用嵌入notebook实验环节的脚手架式AI学习教练（提供分步提示、不生成代码、限制每节提示次数、要求课后简短反思），另一组自由选用自选AI工具。通过作业成绩、问卷与访谈收集数据。

Result: 两组作业成绩无显著差异。教练组信心更高，但提示预算管理不佳；自由组对工具满意，却对自己对AI的依赖感到不安。两组学生均认为"意识到自身对AI的依赖"是课程最有价值的收获。自行制定AI使用规则的学生表现更好；对模型原理理解最深入（且均为自学）的学生使用最审慎、得分最高。

Conclusion: 工具设计的重要性低于学生自我治理AI使用的能力，而这一能力目前只能靠偶然获得。作者主张采取结构性应对：评估应考察AI辅助工作背后的推理过程，并将AI素养作为核心技能显性教授。

Abstract: Generative AI has become a routine resource in programming education, and most institutional responses to it are attempts at control, either by restricting access or by offering students a controlled version of the technology. This paper reports a seven-week mixed-methods pilot study in a master's-level data analytics course, in which 33 students were randomly assigned either to a scaffolded AI Study Coach embedded in the notebook-based laboratory sessions or to unrestricted use of AI tools of their own choosing. The Coach offered stepwise hints, did not generate code, limited the number of hints per session, and required a short reflection at the end of each session. The design assumed, in line with scaffolding theory and recent experimental evidence, that guided and limited support would build confidence and reduce over-reliance, and that the scaffolded group would learn more. Assignment performance did not differ between the conditions. Students in the Coach condition reported higher confidence but managed the hint budget poorly, while students in the unrestricted condition were satisfied with their tools and uneasy about how much they depended on them. In interviews, students in both conditions identified awareness of their own reliance on AI as the most valuable outcome of the course. Students who had formulated their own rules for when to use AI performed better in both conditions, and those with the best understanding of how the models work, in every case self-taught, used the tools most deliberately and achieved the highest scores. The design of the tool mattered less than the students' capacity to govern their own use of it, a capacity that is at present acquired by chance. The paper argues that the appropriate response is structural: assessment that grades the reasoning behind AI-assisted work, and AI literacy taught explicitly as a core skill.

</details>


### [88] [Finding Common Mistakes In Modelling With Mathematical Formalisms Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17111)
*Lilian Killich,Marko Schmellenkamp,Fabian Vehlken,Thomas Zeume*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种工具支持的工作流，利用LLM生成bug修复变换来识别、聚类并可视化学生数学建模中的常见错误，并在命题逻辑等多种形式化中验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生用逻辑公式、数学方程、正则表达式等形式化语言建模时容易出错，识别常见错误有助于提供有针对性的高质量反馈（如交互式学习系统）。手工识别错误在大规模教育数据上难以扩展。

Method: 提出工具支持的工作流：(1)在大规模教育数据集中识别可解释众多学生错误的候选常见错误；(2)按相似性对候选进行聚类；(3)为教师和CS教育研究者可视化聚类。候选错误用bug修复变换表示（将错误形式化转换为正确形式化），由LLM生成并经算法验证。

Result: 成功复现文献中手工识别的命题逻辑建模常见错误；证明相比其他算法方法，LLM方法适合超大数据集；并在多种其他形式化上验证了方法的泛化性。

Conclusion: 该LLM驱动的工具支持工作流能有效识别、聚类并可视化常见建模错误，具有可扩展性且可推广到命题逻辑之外的多种形式化语言。

Abstract: Modelling with mathematical formalisms like logical formulas, mathematical equations, or regular expressions is an important yet challenging task for students of computer science and other STEM disciplines. Identifying common mistakes occurring in this context is an important step towards helping struggling students by providing targeted high-quality feedback, e.g. in interactive learning systems.
  We present a tool-supported workflow that allows to (1) identify candidates for common mistakes that explain many student mistakes in large educational data sets, (2) cluster candidates according to similarities, and (3) visualize resulting clusters for instructors and CS education researchers. The visualization is designed to help researchers to identify common modelling mistakes. The candidates for common mistakes are represented by bug fixing transformations that translate incorrect formalizations into correct formalizations; they are generated by an LLM and validated algorithmically.
  We show that this approach works well by reproducing common mistakes in propositional logic modelling that were identified by hand in the literature; showing that, unlike other algorithmic approaches, the LLM-based approach is suitable for very large sets of data; and applying it to multiple other formalisms to showcase it generalizes beyond propositional logic.

</details>


### [89] [Evaluating Ambient Clinical Scribes in India: The Need for Multilingual Real-World Clinical Conversation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17355)
*Siddharth D Jaiswal,Krithi S,Ashish Makani,Suvrankar Datta,Sunayana Sitaram,Mohit Jain*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文呼吁为全球南方（尤其印度）环境下的临床文书AI（ACS）建立公开、标准化的评估基础设施，指出现有数据集不适用、评估流程碎片化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACS正被快速部署于印度等资源匮乏的全球南方医疗环境，但这些系统多基于全球北方语音、语言和问诊风格开发。印度临床问诊具备简短、三方参与、多语言、代码混合、噪声大等特征，极易放大ASR与笔记生成错误，亟需评估其安全性、可靠性与适配性。

Method: 混合方法研究：1）系统调查公开的患者-临床医生对话数据集；2）将数据集与印度临床沟通文献中的对话及文化标记进行定量对比；3）对五家在印度和非洲构建及部署ACS的机构进行半结构化访谈。

Result: 印度无公开、大规模、真实世界的ACS基准，现有数据集多为合成数据；全球北方数据集与印度问诊的对话和文化结构显著偏离；各部署机构各自建立专有、不可比的评估管线，生态碎片化，采购缺乏独立可靠依据。

Conclusion: 呼吁建立公开共享、真实世界、多语言的ACS评估基准，并概述该基准所需的属性与政策。

Abstract: Ambient clinical scribes (ACS) are being rapidly deployed at scale across Global South healthcare settings, aiming to reduce clinician documentation time, especially in overburdened environments like India. These ACS are primarily developed or distilled from models built and validated on Global North speech, languages and consultation styles. Indian clinical encounters are brief, triadic, multilingual, code-mixed with low-resource languages, and conducted in highly resource-constrained, noisy settings -- increasing the likelihood of ASR and note-generation errors manyfold. We posit an urgent need to develop a standardized evaluation infrastructure to assess whether these systems are safe, reliable, and well-suited to the Indian healthcare setting. We substantiate our claims through a mixed-methods study -- a systematic survey of publicly available patient-clinician conversational datasets, a quantitative comparison of these datasets against conversational and cultural markers drawn from the Indian clinical-communication literature, and semi-structured interviews with five organizations building and deploying ACS in India and Africa. Our survey shows that there are no publicly available, large-scale, real-world benchmarks for ACS in India, with existing datasets being overwhelmingly synthetic. We note that the available Global North datasets diverge significantly from the expected conversational and cultural structures of Indian encounters. Finally, our interviews reveal that deploying organizations have each built proprietary, incomparable evaluation pipelines, creating a fragmented ecosystem with no independent and reliable basis for procurement. We call for the development of a publicly shared, real-world, multilingual benchmark for ACS evaluation and outline the properties and policies such a benchmark would require.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.TR'></div>

# q-fin.TR [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [From Public Evidence to Contractual Outcome: First and Stable Decidability on Kalshi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16642)
*Maksym Nechepurenko*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文提出两种Kalshi市场可判定性时钟（首次可判定性与稳定可判定性），通过回顾性与前瞻性观测测试，得到的是"可观测性"结论而非市场可判定性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测市场合约可在平台首次裁定前，凭公开证据已足以结算，但该边界取决于规则版本、具体发布对象、来源层级、更正历史和未完成的合约条件，故需精确定义"可判定"的时间边界。

Method: 定义两种时钟：首次可判定性（规则—证据映射中最早的同期单例）与稳定可判定性（事后最早、且此后单例保持不变的时点）。进行回顾性识别测试、冻结盲试点、全历史恢复（152,694个ticker、11,530个事件身份、6,540次只读官方请求），以及前瞻性基础设施试运行（3个来源计划、25个市场）。

Result: 盲试点25个身份及盲检全部通过，当前规则文本全部恢复；但无可恢复的历史规则版本与官方发布对象。全历史恢复中历史合格事件与合格ticker均为零。前瞻性试运行中，781,266个生命周期帧通过完整性再验证，22个重连收据闭合，无未解决重连缺口、无逾期未发官方发布，价格层未启用。

Conclusion: 结果属于可观测性结论，并不主张任何市场不可判定或公开证据不存在。前瞻样本仅在入组关闭时基于前瞻冻结身份与结果前字段构建，目标规模按登记的full/reduced/exploratory/no-go支持状态机械选定。本文不报告任何合约可判定性时钟、人工裁决、价格或跨场馆结果。

Abstract: Public evidence can become sufficient to settle a prediction-market contract before the venue records its first determination, but the relevant boundary depends on the applicable rule version, exact release object, source hierarchy, correction history, and unfinished contract conditions. This paper defines two Kalshi clocks: first decidability, the earliest contemporaneous singleton in the rule-evidence mapping, and stable decidability, the retrospective earliest time after which the same singleton remains unchanged through finalization. A completed retrospective identification test establishes a narrow feasibility result. In a frozen blind pilot, all 25 identities and blinding checks passed and current rule text was recovered for all 25; no exact or bounded historical rule version and no exact or bounded official source-release object was recovered. The full historical recovery covered 152,694 ordinary tickers, 11,530 exact event identities, and 6,540 read-only official requests, with zero historically eligible events and zero historically eligible tickers. This is an observability result, not a claim that no market was decidable or that public evidence never existed. A completed prospective infrastructure shakedown established observation capability for three source programmes across 25 markets, with integrity revalidation of 781,266 lifecycle frames, 22 closed lower-bounded reconnect receipts, no unresolved reconnect gap, no due-but-missed official release, and an inactive price layer. Production evidence enrollment is active. The prospective sample is constructed only at enrollment close from prospectively frozen identities and pre-outcome fields; its frozen target size is selected mechanically under the registered full, reduced, exploratory, or no-go support disposition. No contractual-decidability clock, human-adjudication, price, or cross-venue result is reported here.

</details>

### [91] [Financial Contagion Networks as Annealing-Ready Ising Systems Cascades, Bailout Optimization, and Susceptibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17415)
*Abhinav Tomar,Lakshya Nagpal,Vikas Chauhan,S. R. Hassan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于 Ising 模型与 QUBO 的统一优化框架，用于金融网络系统性风险分析与干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融网络通过交叉持股和非线性传染易发生级联失效，系统性风险的分析与缓解在计算上极具挑战。

Method: 从 Elliott-Golub-Jackson 模型出发，将均衡估值扩展到阈值触发失效，建立最大级联失效问题的等价 QUBO；将最优救助分配设为受控 Ising 模型，并把双层优化转为单一联合 QUBO；提出救助敏感度指标和敏感度驱动的贪心干预策略。

Result: 数值模拟在不同规模金融网络上验证了均衡估值、最坏级联识别、最优救助分配和敏感度分析的有效性。

Conclusion: Ising 表示为金融传染提供统计力学框架，所提框架兼容经典退火、量子启发优化及新兴量子退火技术。

Abstract: Interconnected financial systems are vulnerable to cascading failures arising from cross-holdings and nonlinear contagion, making the analysis and mitigation of systemic risk a challenging computational problem. In this work, we develop a unified optimization framework for financial network analysis based on Ising models and Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO). Starting from the Elliott Golub Jackson financial network model, we extend equilibrium valuation to incorporate threshold-induced failures, formulate the Maximum Cascade Failure Problem, and derive an equivalent QUBO representation. We then formulate the Optimal Bailout Allocation Problem as a controlled Ising model and transform the resulting bi-level optimization into a single joint QUBO that simultaneously determines equilibrium failures and optimal interventions under budget constraints. To characterize the influence of individual institutions, we introduce bailout susceptibility as a response-based measure of systemic importance and develop a susceptibility-driven greedy intervention strategy. Numerical simulations demonstrate equilibrium valuation, worst-case cascade identification, optimal bailout allocation, and susceptibility analysis on financial networks of varying sizes. Beyond optimization, the Ising representation provides a general statistical-mechanical framework for analyzing financial contagion, enabling the application of response theory, Monte Carlo methods, and other techniques developed for interacting spin systems. The proposed framework establishes a unified approach for systemic risk analysis that is compatible with classical annealing, quantum-inspired optimization, and emerging quantum annealing technologies

</details>


### [92] [Asymmetric Weighted Earliness-Tardiness: Scheduling with a Nonrestrictive Common Due Date](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16046)
*Nicholas G. Hall,Hans Kellerer,Miao Song*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究单机非对称加权提前-拖期(AWET)调度问题，在非限制性同步日期d=Σp_j下将问题化为对非空提前集的二次选择，并给出强NP完全性、近似算法、伪多项式精确算法与FPTAS四类互补结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AWET问题中提前与拖期惩罚互不相关且依赖于工件，如何在共同同步日期附近排序使总惩罚最小是核心难题；作者希望厘清决定问题复杂性的关键因素——不仅是两个比率序是否一致，还在于其交互能否被全局证书控制或压缩为有界构造接口。

Method: 在边界非限制日期下利用紧凑V形调度将连续时间问题归约为提前集选择。(1)由Restricted Exact Cover by 3-Sets做一元多项式归约证明正整数情形强NP完全；(2)基于锚定半定松弛与确定性边际阈值给出(3+2√2+ε)近似；(3)当提前/拖期比率序严格互逆时给出两资源伪多项式动态规划；(4)对固定总细化且比率置换可分离情形，用分离树递推与协调几何剪枝构造FPTAS。证明统一使用尺度分离前缀惩罚、半正定最小核协方差、主导完工载荷平方及有界四坐标分解接口。

Result: 正整数AWET为强NP完全；无限制AWET存在多项式时间(3+2√2+ε)近似；比率序严格互逆时为弱NP完全并可在伪多项式时间内精确求解；固定总细化且置换可分离时存在FPTAS。

Conclusion: AWET问题的决定性不在于两个比率序是否一致，而在于其交互能否被全局证书控制或压缩为有界构造接口；四种情形展示了同一标准目标的不同表现形式。

Abstract: Single-machine asymmetric weighted earliness--tardiness (AWET) scheduling asks how to sequence jobs around a common synchronization date when early and late completion incur unrelated job-dependent penalties. At the boundary nonrestrictive date $d=\sum_jp_j$, a compact V-shaped schedule reduces the continuous-time problem to a quadratic choice of a nonempty early set. We establish four complementary results for this model. First, the positive-integer problem is strongly NP-complete by a unary-polynomial reduction from Restricted Exact Cover by 3-Sets. Second, unrestricted AWET admits a polynomial-time $(3+2\sqrt2+\varepsilon)$-approximation based on an anchored semidefinite relaxation and deterministic marginal thresholding. Third, when the earliness and tardiness ratio orders are strict reversals, the problem is weakly NP-complete but has an exact two-resource pseudopolynomial dynamic program. Fourth, for fixed total refinements whose ratio permutation is separable, an exact separating-tree recurrence and coordinated geometric trimming yield an FPTAS. The proofs use different manifestations of the same canonical objective: scale-separated prefix penalties, positive-semidefinite minimum-kernel covariance, a dominant completed load square, and a bounded four-coordinate decomposition interface. Together, the results show that the decisive issue is not merely whether the two ratio orders agree, but whether their interaction can be controlled by a global certificate or compressed into a bounded constructive interface.

</details>


### [93] [The fixed-point bundle method over product-of-simplex domains arising from game equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16158)
*Hongbo Sun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 将有限维变分不等式的固定点丛框架从单纯形域推广到单纯形积域，用于求解Nash均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有固定点丛框架仅适用于单纯形域，无法直接求解Nash均衡等单纯形积域问题。

Method: 揭示单纯形积域上VI的复合纤维丛结构，构造等价单纯形域VI，并通过纤维丛同构建立两个框架的等价性。

Result: 预测-校正路径跟踪算法继承单纯形域框架的收敛保证：全局收敛且在解附近线性间隙缩减；5600个随机实例（2玩家128动作到128玩家2动作）全部收敛。

Conclusion: 几何等价性使单纯形积域VI算法获得理论收敛保证并在实验中表现稳健。

Abstract: This paper extends the fixed-point bundle framework for finite-dimensional variational inequalities (VIs) from the simplex domain to the product-of-simplex domain, which is directly applicable to solving Nash equilibria. The fixed-point bundle for VIs on the product-of-simplex domain reveals a composite fiber bundle structure. The key innovation is to construct an equivalent VI on the simplex domain and establish the equivalence between the two fixed-point bundle frameworks via a fiber bundle isomorphism. Exploiting this geometric equivalence, the predictor-corrector path-following algorithm for the VI on the product-of-simplex domain is shown to inherit the convergence guarantee of the simplex-domain framework, namely, global convergence with linear gap reduction near solutions. Numerical experiments on 5600 randomly generated instances with dimensions ranging from 2-player 128-action to 128-player 2-action demonstrate robust performance. The algorithm converges in every tested instance.

</details>


### [94] [GPU-Accelerated Presolving for Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16182)
*Daniel Cederberg,Stephen Boyd*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文挑战"presolve 本质串行、难以并行"的传统观点，提出 GPU 加速的 presolver cuPSLP，在两大 LP 基准集上将 presolve 时间相比 PSLP 缩减 11x 和 42x，且归约质量相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LP 求解器的核心算法已实现 GPU 加速，但 presolve 等阶段仍停留在 CPU，成为整个求解流程日益严重的瓶颈。学界与工业界长期认为 presolve 本质串行且不规则，难以高效并行，因此少有 GPU 化尝试。本文旨在挑战并推翻这一观点。

Method: 提出避免严重负载不均衡、并挖掘"看似串行"归约中并行性的简单设计原则；据此实现 cuPSLP——将基于 CPU 的 PSLP（本身已比最先进商业 presolver 快数倍）移植为 GPU 加速版本。

Result: 在 Mittelmann LP 基准集上，cuPSLP 将 PSLP 的 shifted geometric mean presolve 时间缩减 11 倍；在 GAMS 大规模 LP 基准集上缩减 42 倍，且归约质量相近。

Conclusion: presolve 可显著受益于 GPU 加速，传统"固有串行、不规则"的观点并不成立，GPU 化 presolve 是突破完整求解流程瓶颈的可行方向。

Abstract: Recent research has focused on developing GPU-accelerated algorithms for solving linear programs (LPs), with results that are nothing short of extraordinary. But a complete solver pipeline consists of more than the core algorithm, and the elephant in the room is that stages such as presolve have remained on the CPU, where they have become an increasingly large bottleneck. Presolve has stayed on the CPU because of the longstanding view, held by academic and industrial developers alike, that it is inherently sequential and irregular, and therefore difficult to parallelize efficiently. In this paper we challenge this view and show that presolving can benefit substantially from GPU acceleration. We describe simple design principles that avoid severe load imbalance and expose parallelism in reductions that appear inherently sequential. We implement these principles in cuPSLP, a GPU-accelerated version of PSLP, a CPU-based presolver that is itself several times faster than a state-of-the-art commercial presolver. In our experiments, cuPSLP reduces the shifted geometric mean presolve time of PSLP by a factor of 11 on the Mittelmann LP benchmark set and 42 on the GAMS large-scale LP benchmark set, with similar reduction quality.

</details>


### [95] [Dimensional Decomposition and Column Generation for Bin Dimensioning in E-Commerce Fulfillment Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16272)
*Gabriel González,Mariá C. V. Nascimento*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究电商履约中心的料箱尺寸设计问题：在候选箱型与异构库存下，决定各箱型数量并分配商品，以最小化总箱体积，同时满足箱内组合的运营约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 料箱尺寸须匹配各设施的商品结构以提升空间利用率与上架/拣选效率，但目前缺乏针对大规模电商库存的料箱尺寸优化方法。

Method: 利用运营约束进行维度分解，将三维装箱约简为一维块定位问题；据此建立混合整数线性规划，并提出Best-Fit-Decreasing启发式与列生成方法以得到紧的LP下界。

Result: 在四个合成数据集（由美国电商金融科技公司专有数据校准，1.5千至150万SKU）上，BFD可扩展到170万块规模；列生成在最小数据集上证明BFD–LP间隙为2.26%；实验揭示由运营约束与商品结构交互导致的数据集间解质量结构性差异。

Conclusion: 维度分解与列生成结合可有效求解大规模料箱尺寸问题，并在实践中量化启发式解质量；但解质量受设施商品结构与运营约束共同影响而呈结构性分化。

Abstract: A fulfillment center (FC) is a specialized logistics facility where e-commerce inventory is received, stored, and processed. Its internal organization directly impacts space utilization and the performance of put-away and picking operations. Items are stored in bins following strict operational guidelines to ensure productivity in both activities, so bin dimensions must be tailored to the product profile of each facility to maximize space utilization. %
We address the bin dimensioning problem arising in e-commerce fulfillment centers: given a set of candidate bin types and a large, heterogeneous inventory, determine the number of bins of each type and assign all items so as to minimize total bin volume, subject to operational constraints governing how products may be combined within a single bin. We develop a dimensional decomposition that exploits these constraints to reduce the three-dimensional packing problem to a one-dimensional block-positioning problem. Building on this decomposition, we formulate the problem as a mixed-integer linear program and propose two solution methods: a Best-Fit-Decreasing (BFD) heuristic and a column generation scheme that produces tight LP lower bounds. Computational experiments on four synthetic datasets, calibrated on proprietary data from an American e-commerce fintech and ranging from 1.5 thousand to 1.5 million SKUs, show that the BFD heuristic scales to instances with up to 1.7 million blocks, and that column generation certifies a BFD--LP gap of 2.26\% on the smallest dataset. The experiments further reveal a structural divide in solution quality across datasets, driven by the interaction between operational constraints and each facility's product profile.

</details>


### [96] [Beyond Phase Reduction: Amplitude Collapse in Optimal Control of Coupled Oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16449)
*Faranak Rajabi,Frédéric Gibou,Jeff Moehlis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文求解二维扩散耦合Stuart-Landau振子的四维HJB方程，发现强耦合下最优控制策略从相位重定位转为瞬时振幅塌缩，揭示了偏离极限环的非常规控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合振子的相位协调控制常依赖降阶相位模型，但相位模型在强耦合下准确性不足，可能遗漏全状态最优策略。作者希望获得全状态最优反馈控制并检验降阶模型假设。

Method: 对两个扩散耦合的Stuart-Landau类振子求解四维HJB方程，得到全状态最优反馈控制；扫描耦合强度、非等时性等参数，并与梯度优化、随机优化、无耦合及耦合相位降阶基线、Van der Pol振子进行对比。

Result: 存在一个阈值耦合：低于阈值时控制器将相位差驱向反相并保持振子靠近极限环；高于阈值时控制器先令一个振子振幅塌缩至近零，在原点附近完成大幅相位重定位后再恢复振幅。该低代价轨迹难以被直接梯度或随机优化找到。即使等时振子也可发生塌缩；非等时性通过小振幅相位速度盈余进一步降低控制代价。强耦合下相位模型误差大、能耗更高。Van der Pol振子同样利用离环动力学。

Conclusion: 强耦合下最优相位控制可依赖离极限环的振幅塌缩策略，降阶相位模型不可靠且更耗能；离环控制策略在非等时及不同振子模型中具有普适性。

Abstract: We solve the four-dimensional Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equation for two diffusively coupled Stuart-Landau-like oscillators to obtain full-state optimal feedback control. A sweep over coupling strength reveals a sharp change in the numerically optimal strategy: below a threshold coupling value, the controller steers the phase difference toward anti-phase while keeping both oscillators near the limit cycle, as reduced-order models would suggest. Above this threshold, the HJB solution changes qualitatively; the controller transiently collapses one oscillator's amplitude to near zero, thereby enabling large phase repositioning near the origin before rebuilding its amplitude. Direct gradient-based and stochastic optimization do not recover this lower-cost collapse trajectory from the initializations considered, suggesting that it occupies a region of the control landscape that is difficult to access by direct search. A joint sweep over nonisochronicity and coupling shows that collapse can occur even for an isochronous oscillator: phase repositioning near the origin can favor an off-cycle strategy. Nonisochronicity provides an additional energetic benefit through a phase-velocity surplus at small amplitude, quantitatively accounting for the observed reduction in control cost. Comparisons with uncoupled and coupled phase-reduced baselines show that phase models become increasingly inaccurate and cost significantly more energy for strong coupling. Results for coupled Van der Pol oscillators further demonstrate that exploitation of off-cycle dynamics is not specific to the Stuart-Landau-like oscillators.

</details>


### [97] [Master Equations for Mean Field Game of Controls: A Unification of Weak Solution Notions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16524)
*Mengzhen Li,Xintian Liu,Chenchen Mou,Zhen Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究控制型平均场博弈的主方程，在仅假设数据对测度变量Lipschitz连续时提出"弱解"概念并证明其全局适定性，最后统一了文献中多种非光滑解概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主方程理论通常要求数据对测度变量具有更强正则性（如可微性），而仅Lipschitz连续的情形缺乏系统处理。作者希望在较弱假设下建立解的理论。

Method: 针对仅Lipschitz连续的数据，提出更弱的"弱解"概念；通过分析相应的Pontryagin前向后向随机微分方程组，特别是测度变量方向的稳定性来证明全局适定性，分别在Lasry-Lions单调性和位移λ-单调性条件下进行。

Result: 在Lasry-Lions单调性和位移λ-单调性条件下分别建立了主方程的全局适定性；并证明文献中的good solutions、weak viscosity solutions、Lipschitz solutions、monotone solutions在适当假设下与本文弱解彼此等价。

Conclusion: 本文为控制型平均场博弈主方程的各种弱解概念提供了统一框架，表明这些概念在适当条件下本质相同。

Abstract: In this manuscript, we study the master equation for mean field game of controls, assuming only that the data are Lipschitz continuous in the measure variable. Accordingly, we propose a weaker notion of solution called weak solutions for the master equation. We establish the global well-posedness of the master equation for such solution under the Lasry-Lions monotonicity and displacement $λ$-monotonicity conditions, respectively. The arguments rely on the analysis of the associated Pontryagin forward-backward stochastic differential equation system, especially the stability in the measure variable. Finally, we review several existing notions of non-smooth solution from the literature, namely good solutions, weak viscosity solutions, Lipschitz solutions, monotone solutions, and examine their relationship with our weak solution. We show that under appropriate assumptions all of them are equivalent by their definitions, and thus our manuscript provides a unification of weak solution notions for the master equations derived from mean field game of controls.

</details>


### [98] [Stability Analysis of Generalized Multi-Source Weber Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16703)
*N. H. Hai,V. S. T. Long,N. M. Nam,N. D. Yen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在Minkowski函数框架下研究数据扰动对广义多源Weber问题稳定性的影响，给出Lipschitz常数、解集性质及连续性的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究数据扰动下广义多源Weber问题（在Minkowski函数框架内）的稳定性，回答关于局部最优解映射稳定性的若干开放问题。

Method: 建立目标函数和最优值函数的显式Lipschitz常数；证明全局最优解集的性质；给出全局解映射上半连续性和局部解映射内半连续性的充分条件；构造三个示例进行说明。

Result: 得到了目标函数与最优值函数的显式Lipschitz常数，证明了全局最优解集的若干性质，并给出全局解映射上半连续和局部解映射内半连续的充分条件，辅以三个示例。

Conclusion: 研究结果间接回答了关于所讨论优化问题局部最优解映射稳定性的若干开放问题。

Abstract: This paper presents a stability analysis of the generalized multi-source Weber problems under the influence of data perturbation within the framework of the Minkowski function. First, we establish explicit Lipschitz constants for the objective functions and optimal value functions. Then, we prove several properties of the global optimal solution sets. Furthermore, we provide sufficient conditions for the upper semicontinuity of global solution mappings and the inner semicontinuity of local solution mappings. Three illustrative examples are constructed. Our results give indirect answers to several open questions regarding the stability of local optimal solution mappings of the optimization problems discussed.

</details>


### [99] [Penalty-Uniform Localization for State-Constrained Policy Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16829)
*Yeongjong Kim,Jiwoong Jang,Yeoneung Kim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究带状态约束的确定性折扣最优控制中惩罚项导致的数值泄漏与定位问题，证明惩罚本身提供边界约束力，可控制泄漏代价并给出定位误差界，进而证明神经值近似在耦合细化下收敛到约束值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 惩罚型状态约束会产生奇异定位问题：全空间值与惩罚参数倒数同阶增长，而数值扩散使内向反馈也可能越过边界。需澄清惩罚、网格与数值扩散如何相互作用。

Method: 针对确定性折扣最优控制，采用单调中心差分与消失粘性的格式，用惩罚项提供内向势垒；分析网格h与惩罚ε下的泄漏代价O(h/ε)；在h≤ε下用占据估计得到O(h)定位误差；推广到有界平方距离惩罚，并结合残差评估界与折扣策略误差传播，给出耦合细化下神经值近似收敛条件。

Result: 内向势垒将数值泄漏附加代价限制为O(h/ε)；h≤ε时有O(h)定位误差，所需箱裕度对1/h对数依赖且与ε无关；在惩罚与离散误差消失的耦合细化下，评估误差与贪心间隙的显式条件保证神经值近似收敛到约束值，允许多值贪心选择；圆柱基准检验到20维，障碍导航实验表明需同时评估策略值精度与采样残差。

Conclusion: 惩罚本身提供的约束力可抵消全空间值增长，控制泄漏并实现O(h)定位；结合残差与策略误差传播可保证神经值收敛，数值实验支持理论与基准。

Abstract: Penalizing a state constraint creates a singular localization problem: whole-space values may grow like the inverse penalty parameter, while numerical diffusion allows even an inward feedback to cross the boundary. For deterministic discounted optimal control, we show how the penalty itself supplies confinement that offsets this growth. With mesh size $h$ and penalty parameter $\varepsilon$, an inward barrier bounds the additional cost of numerical leakage by $O(h/\varepsilon)$ for a monotone centered-difference scheme with vanishing viscosity. Under $h\le\varepsilon$, an occupation estimate then yields $O(h)$ localization error with a sufficient box margin logarithmic in $1/h$ and independent of $\varepsilon$. We extend the result to bounded squared-distance penalties and combine it with residual-based evaluation bounds and discounted policy-error propagation. Under coupled refinement with vanishing penalization and discretization errors, explicit conditions on evaluation errors and greedy gaps ensure convergence of the neural value approximations to the constrained value, allowing measurable, nonunique greedy selectors. Reference calculations isolate leakage and localization across mesh and penalty scales, and distinguish evaluation, iteration, and approximation errors. An explicit cylindrical state-constraint solution provides a benchmark in arbitrary dimension, tested up to dimension twenty. Paired obstacle-navigation experiments illustrate why policy-value accuracy must be assessed alongside sampled residuals when comparing raw-residual and finite-grid-assisted neural evaluation.

</details>


### [100] [Integrating Wildfire Risk into Robust Forest Management Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16865)
*Clémence Labarre,Denis Loustau,Jean-Christophe Domec,Mengistie Kindu,Thomas Knoke*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合火灾模拟与生长模型的优化方法，用于在火灾风险加剧下优化人工林景观管理，发现纳入火灾不确定性会显著改变管理策略，倾向多样化组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端天气（如高强度森林火灾）日益严重且难以预测，威胁长期生态系统服务，亟需重新评估现有森林管理规划；现有规划框架少有纳入考虑极端事件不确定性的优化方法，且过程模型的潜力尚未被充分用于战略管理决策。

Method: 聚焦集约人工林，提出一种组合方法：在生长模型内嵌入火灾模拟，对火灾风险不断上升的森林景观管理进行优化。

Result: 纳入火灾行为及其不确定性会显著改变管理结果，倾向于多样化组合——在高产但易火的管理模式与低风险替代方案间取得平衡。多样化能提升木材供给等目标的稳健性，但不保证碳储存也获得协同收益。

Conclusion: 该方法揭示了现有管理制度的局限，并指出改进规划的关键方向，以更好地缓解火灾风险、增强长期韧性。

Abstract: Increasingly severe and unpredictable extreme weather events-such as high-intensity forest firesthreaten long-term ecosystem service provision, driving a critical reassessment of current forest management planning. While planning frameworks exist, few incorporate optimization methods that account for the uncertainty and variability introduced by such extremes. Meanwhile, process-based models have advanced our understanding of fire impacts on forest dynamics and ecosystem services, yet their potential to inform strategic management decisions remains largely untapped. Focusing on intensive plantation forests, we propose a combined approach that uses fire simulation within a growth model to optimize forested landscape management under increasing fire risk. We demonstrate that incorporating fire behavior and its uncertainties significantly alters management outcomes, favoring diversified portfolios that balance high-yield but fire-prone regimes with lowerrisk alternatives. While diversification improves robustness for objectives like timber provision, it does not guarantee complementary benefits for carbon storage. Our method exposes the limitations of current management regimes and identifies key areas for improved planning to better mitigate fire risk and strengthen long-term resilience.

</details>


### [101] [Static output-feedback stabilization is NP-hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16886)
*Johan Löfberg*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明无限制静态输出反馈镇定问题是NP难的，即使输入为单输入多输出（SIMO）情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定静态输出反馈镇定的计算复杂度是控制理论中的核心问题；此前下界多依赖结构化限制或增益约束，本文旨在给出无限制实数增益下的强NP难性结果。

Method: 从NP完全的Betweenness问题出发，构造多项式时间多一归约。给定n个元素、m个约束，构造整数矩阵A∈Z^{D×D}、B∈Z^{D×1}、C∈Z^{n×D}，其中D=6(m+n)，矩阵元素位长为对数级。利用一个通用三次源多项式的Routh行列式强制符号条件，用固定锚值排除任意大的虚假镇定增益，再通过频率分离将多个源条件压缩为公共仿射多项式，并实现为A+BKC的特征多项式。

Result: Betweenness实例可满足当且仅当存在实行向量K∈R^{1×n}使A+BKC为Hurwitz。因此无限制静态输出反馈镇定是NP难的。副产品：判断仿射参数化有理多项式族在有理盒内是否包含Hurwitz成员也是NP难的。

Conclusion: 即便在SIMO且增益无限制的简单设置下，静态输出反馈镇定的判定仍是NP难的，说明该问题的本质计算困难性。

Abstract: We prove that unrestricted static output-feedback stabilization is NP-hard, already in the single-input multiple-output case. More precisely, we give a polynomial-time many-one reduction from the NP-complete Betweenness problem. Given an instance with $n$ elements and $m$ betweenness constraints, the reduction constructs integer matrices $A\in\mathbb Z^{D\times D}$, $B\in\mathbb Z^{D\times1}$, and $C\in\mathbb Z^{n\times D}$ such that the instance is satisfiable if and only if there exists an unrestricted real row vector $K\in\mathbb{R}^{1\times n}$ for which $A+BKC$ is Hurwitz. The state dimension is $D=6(m+n)$, and every matrix entry has logarithmic bit length in the input size. The reduction uses one universal cubic source polynomial whose Routh determinant enforces the sign condition encoding betweenness, while fixed anchor values provide a mechanism for excluding spurious stabilizing gains of arbitrarily large magnitude. Frequency separation then compresses the simultaneous source conditions into one common affine polynomial, which is realized directly as the characteristic polynomial of $A+BKC$. As a by-product, deciding whether an affinely parameterized rational polynomial family contains a Hurwitz member on a rational box is NP-hard.

</details>


### [102] [Invariance of Hurwitz-Stability of Polynomials of Degree Five under Positive Hadamard Powers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16920)
*Rola Alseidi,Raoufah Alsaidi,Jürgen Garloff*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了五次首一 Hurwitz 稳定多项式 f 的分数阶 Hadamard 幂的保稳定性指数集，证明存在唯一阈值 p_*(f)∈(0,1)，使得 p 次 Hadamard 幂 Hurwitz 稳定当且仅当 p>p_*(f)，即保稳定性指数集恰为 (p_*(f),∞)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多项式 Hadamard 幂何时保持 Hurwitz 稳定性是一个经典问题，但分数阶指数情形在次数较低时仍缺乏完整刻画；五次情形尤为关键，因为它是首个不能由低次结果直接推出、且依赖于多个参数的非平凡情形。

Method: 将稳定性问题归约为一个只依赖于由 f 的系数构成的三个参数的标量函数，通过分析该单变量函数确定稳定性的临界条件，从而得到唯一阈值 p_*(f)；并进一步研究该阈值对参数的依赖性。

Result: 证明存在唯一阈值 p_*(f)∈(0,1)，使得 f 的 p 次 Hadamard 幂 Hurwitz 稳定当且仅当 p>p_*(f)，因此保稳定性指数集为 (p_*(f),∞)；该阈值光滑依赖于三个参数，并在可行参数区域上给出一个整体坐标；最后用一族复共轭零点趋近虚轴的单参数 Hurwitz 稳定多项式示例说明这种光滑依赖性。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了五次首一 Hurwitz 稳定多项式分数阶 Hadamard 幂的保稳定性指数集刻画问题，揭示出阈值 p_*(f) 作为参数区域整体坐标的几何结构，并通过逼近虚轴的零点族展示了阈值的连续与光滑行为。

Abstract: A complete characterization of the stability-preserving exponent set for fractional Hadamard powers of a monic Hurwitz-stable polynomial f of degree five is presented. The stability problem is reduced to the analysis of a single scalar function depending only on three parameters formed from the coefficients of the polynomial f. This reduction leads to a unique stability threshold $p_*(f) \in (0,1)$ such that the $p$-th Hadamard power of $f$ is Hurwitz stable if and only if $p > p_*(f)$. Consequently, the stability-preserving exponent set is precisely $(p_*(f),\infty)$. This threshold depends smoothly on the parameters and provides a global coordinate on the admissible parameter region. Finally, smooth dependence is illustrated by a one-parameter family of Hurwitz-stable polynomials whose complex-conjugate zeros approach the imaginary axis.

</details>


### [103] [Learning Choice Model Trees for Feature-Based Multi-Product Pricing: Exact Optimization and Field Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16952)
*Jiajie Zhang,Yanqiu Ruan,Xiao Jin,Chung Piaw Teo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出OCMT-MNL，用动态规划联合优化树结构与MNL叶子模型，显著加速最优决策树构建并提升定价收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于特征的多产品定价中，选择模型树通过可解释规则分割客户以捕捉需求异质性，但通常采用贪婪逐次分割，容易陷入局部最优。作者希望在同一深度约束下联合优化树结构与叶子需求模型，获得更优定价策略。

Method: OCMT-MNL在指定深度内联合优化树和带多项Logit叶子的模型；使用精确动态规划，在约束牛顿迭代中推导闭式Fenchel下界，并在嵌套与不相交客户子集间传播下界，从而避免重复拟合、可恢复未完成拟合。还构建一维查表将离线估计转为实时定价，并给出网格间距二次的收益损失界。

Result: 合成实验中，精确叶子拟合减少99.98%，叶子评估减少86.13%，较未剪枝动态规划最高加速7.15倍；相比贪婪树，合成数据上以更少叶子获得更低收益损失，真实数据上预测拟合更好。在48个航空市场、190,220名乘客、23周随机实验中，辅助座位每乘客收入较静态定价显著提升11.3%。

Conclusion: OCMT-MNL能高效构建最优选择模型树，并在真实定价实验中显著提升收益，兼具计算加速、可解释性与实时部署潜力。

Abstract: Feature-based multi-product pricing uses customer characteristics to identify demand heterogeneity and tailor prices across products. Choice model trees segment customers through interpretable feature rules and fit a demand model within each leaf. Existing methods typically construct these trees greedily, selecting one myopic split at a time. We develop optimal choice model trees with multinomial logit leaves (OCMT-MNL), jointly optimizing the tree and leaf models within a prescribed depth. Our exact dynamic program derives closed-form Fenchel lower bounds during constrained Newton iterations and propagates them across nested and disjoint customer subsets, avoiding new fits and resuming unfinished fits without repeating completed work. In synthetic experiments, it reduces exact leaf fits by 99.98% and leaf evaluations by 86.13%, achieving up to 7.15-fold speedups over unpruned dynamic programming. One-dimensional lookup tables translate offline estimation into real-time pricing, with a revenue-loss bound quadratic in grid spacing under the fitted model. Compared with greedy trees, OCMT-MNL achieves lower revenue loss with fewer leaves on synthetic data and better predictive fit on real data. In a 23-week randomized experiment on ancillary seat pricing across 48 airline markets and 190,220 passengers, OCMT-MNL increases seat revenue per passenger by a statistically significant 11.3% over static pricing.

</details>


### [104] [The Unified Separation Condition: A General Constraint Qualification for Smooth and Nonsmooth Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16981)
*Majid E. Abbasov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出统一分离条件(USC)，作为有限维非线性规划的约束限定条件，统一并推广经典MFCQ/LICQ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典约束限定条件（MFCQ、LICQ等）多针对光滑情形，在非光滑优化中不适用，且缺乏统一框架，因此需要一个更一般、更简单的约束限定条件。

Method: 定义USC：要求原点在可行集邻域内与约束违反函数的次微分一致分离。证明不等式MFCQ与等式LICQ均可推出USC；并引入USC的局部版本，讨论通过凸二次规划进行可计算验证。

Result: MFCQ⇒USC（基于Gordan定理）；LICQ⇒USC（基于活跃约束梯度的线性无关）。USC严格更一般：在经典条件未定义的非光滑场景仍适用，且可在MFCQ失效时成立。

Conclusion: USC为约束限定条件提供统一框架，弥合经典光滑理论与非光滑优化之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: We introduce the Unified Separation Condition (USC) - a simple and general constraint qualification for finite-dimensional nonlinear programming. The USC requires that the origin be uniformly separated from the subdifferential of the constraint violation function in a neighborhood of the feasible set.
  The main result of this paper is two-fold. First, we show that the classical Mangasarian--Fromovitz constraint qualification (MFCQ) for inequalities and the linear independence constraint qualification (LICQ) for equalities imply the USC. Conversely, USC is strictly more general: it remains applicable in nonsmooth settings where classical conditions are not defined, and it may hold even when MFCQ fails. The proof that MFCQ implies USC is based on Gordan's theorem; the implication from LICQ to USC follows from the linear independence of the gradients of the active constraints.
  We also introduce a local version of USC and discuss its computational verification via convex quadratic programming. The USC provides a unified framework for constraint qualifications, bridging classical smooth theory and nonsmooth optimization.

</details>


### [105] [From Consensus-Based Optimization to Particle Swarm Optimization: Convergence Guarantees under Drift-Diffusion Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17002)
*Franca Hoffmann,Dohyeon Kim,Ritvik Teegavarapu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文分析PSO与CBO的参数耦合对收敛保证的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PSO缺乏严格性能保证且无自然平均场描述。

Method: 修改PSO以恢复带记忆的CBO，分析参数耦合对收敛保证的影响。

Result: 参数耦合仍留有非空可行域，但在恢复PSO的极限下该范围收缩。

Conclusion: CBO的收敛保证不能直接推广到经典PSO，并给出数值模拟验证。

Abstract: Particle swarm optimization (PSO) is a widely used algorithm featured in many state-of-the-art optimization tool-kits. However, rigorous performance guarantees are still lacking. The standard PSO dynamics do not admit a natural mean-field description, which would provide an avenue for theoretical analysis. By modifying the PSO formulation, one can recover the consensus-based optimization (CBO) algorithm with memory, which admits a mean-field limit and facilitates rigorous convergence analysis. These theoretical guarantees rely heavily on the fact that for CBO, the drift and noise strengths can be chosen independently, whereas they are coupled for PSO. We analyze how the PSO parameter coupling affects existing convergence guarantees for CBO and its variant with memory effect. We show, by an explicit construction, that the coupling still leaves a non-empty set of admissible parameters for these convergence guarantees to hold. However, the admissible parameter ranges shrink in the limits used to recover PSO. The resulting convergence guarantees from CBO therefore do not directly extend to the classical PSO model. We provide numerical simulations illustrating the parameter tradeoffs shown in the theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [106] [Toland duality and particle approximations for signed Wasserstein barycenters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17073)
*Thomas Gallouët,Andrea Natale,Gabriele Todeschi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究带符号系数的加权平方Wasserstein距离最小化问题，给出单正系数情形下的对偶形式及一般符号重心算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带符号系数的Wasserstein距离加权和刻画了度量外推等问题，但缺乏系统的对偶理论与数值求解方法。

Method: 利用Toland对偶，借助问题中不同凸性概念，对单个正系数情形导出两种凸对偶形式：基于Brenier势的表述，以及凸序下的投影问题；对一般符号重心给出将其归约到单正系数情形的必要最优性条件；数值上提出基于熵正则化与Sinkhorn迭代的无网格粒子算法。

Result: 得到单正系数情形的两类凸对偶形式，推广了近来建立的度量外推双向情形结果；一般符号重心的必要条件可归约至单正系数问题；算法可计算任意系数的符号重心。

Conclusion: 为带符号系数的Wasserstein重心提供了对偶理论与实用数值方法，拓展了度量外推与符号重心研究。

Abstract: We study the problem of minimizing a weighted sum of squared Wasserstein distances with signed coefficients. In the case of a single positive coefficient, we derive two convex dual formulations: one expressed in terms of Brenier potentials, and one as a projection problem in convex order. Such dual formulations arise via Toland duality by exploiting different notions of convexity within the problem. They generalize those recently established for the metric extrapolation problem, which corresponds to signed barycenters between only two measures. For general signed barycenters, we identify necessary optimality conditions that reduce the problem to the case with one positive coefficient. On the numerical side, we propose a grid-free, particle-based algorithm built on entropic regularization and Sinkhorn iterations to compute signed barycenters with arbitrary coefficients.

</details>


### [107] [Optimization over covariance matrices with a parameterized metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17089)
*Yibang Li,Bamdev Mishra,Pratik Jawanpuria,Cyrus Mostajeran*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文引入一个双参数Riemannian度量族 X^pLX^q+X^qLX^p=U，统一了Euclidean、Bures-Wasserstein和affine-invariant度量，并将其视为针对具体问题的预条件选择，通过分析Riemannian Hessian的条件数来指导调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协方差矩阵上做梯度优化时，度量选择（Euclidean、Bures-Wasserstein、affine-invariant等）对收敛影响很大，但各度量优劣依赖于目标函数，缺乏统一框架来比较和选择合适度量。作者希望把度量选择转化为一种预条件问题，从条件数角度理论指导度量选取与调参。

Method: 定义一个由方程 X^pLX^q+X^qLX^p=U 在每个切向量U处求解L给出的双参数度量族(p,q)，它精确包含三种常见度量（(0,0)、(1,0)、(1,1)）。把成员选择看作针对给定问题的预条件方式，分析解处Riemannian Hessian的条件性：证明其下界仅通过r=p+q依赖于(p,q)；当Euclidean Hessian为纯幂且不混合特征方向时，p=q=r/2达到该下界，并给出闭式判据识别其他达到下界的成员。还讨论了针对具体问题调节r的方法。

Result: 理论证明Riemannian Hessian条件数下界只依赖r=p+q，且p=q=r/2在特定情形下达到下界，并给出闭式判据。真实协方差数据实验验证了预测的条件数行为以及调节r带来的收益；任务协方差示例显示进一步调节形状（p、q的分配）能带来额外增益。

Conclusion: 该双参数度量族为协方差矩阵优化提供了统一框架，将度量选择理解为预条件，并通过条件数理论指导：先调r=p+q，再调形状。实验证明了理论预测与调参收益。

Abstract: The choice of Riemannian metric can strongly influence the convergence of gradient-based optimization over covariance matrices. Euclidean, Bures-Wasserstein and affine-invariant metrics are common choices, but their relative effectiveness depends on the objective. We introduce a two-parameter family defined by $X^{p}LX^{q}+X^{q}LX^{p}=U$, solved for $L$ at each tangent vector $U$, that contains all three as exact members, at $(0,0)$, $(1,0)$ and $(1,1)$, and extends past them. We treat the choice of member as a particular way of preconditioning for a given problem. To this end, we analyze the conditioning of the Riemannian Hessian at the solution. We show that it obeys a lower bound that depends on $(p,q)$ only through the exponent $r=p+q$. When the Euclidean Hessian is a pure power that mixes no eigendirections, the member $p=q=r/2$ attains that bound, and a closed-form criterion identifies the other members that do. We discuss ways to tune $r$ for a given problem. Experiments on real covariance data confirm the predicted conditioning and the benefit of tuning $r$. A task covariance example shows a further gain from tuning the shape.

</details>


### [108] [Intervention problems in the Linear Threshold Model: A general formulation and new results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17146)
*Giacomo Como,Fabio Fagnani,Stephane Durand*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究线性阈值模型的最优干预问题，提出"有向路径数"这一图论量，并证明其与最优干预成本的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何以最小成本修改智能体阈值，使网络全局收敛到全1配置，揭示网络结构对干预成本的刻画。

Method: 在线性阈值模型下，假设规划者以阈值提升总量为成本修改阈值，定义有向路径数（覆盖图所需的最少不相交路径且可定向为DAG），并分析其与最优干预成本的关系。

Result: 当所有阈值为1/2时，最优成本恰等于有向路径数；一般情况下，有向路径数是该最优成本界的主要成分。

Conclusion: 有向路径数是刻画线性阈值模型全局收敛干预成本的关键图论量。

Abstract: We study an optimal intervention problem for linear threshold models. This is a popular class of dynamical network systems whereby a number of agents, identified with the nodes of a graph, strategically change their binary action (0 or 1) according to a threshold rule. Specifically, an agent adopts action 1 if and only if the fraction of its neighbors in the interaction graph that do so is greater than or equal to a prescribed threshold. Assuming that a planner can modify the agents' thresholds at a cost equal to the aggregate threshold increase, we study the minimum intervention cost needed to ensure global convergence to the all-1 configuration. Our main contribution is the introduction of a new graph-theoretic quantity, called oriented path number, that is the minimum number of disjoint paths needed to cover the graph that can be oriented to form a directed acyclic graph. When thresholds are all equal to 1/2, the optimal cost is shown to coincide with the oriented path number, whereas, in the general case, it turns out to be the main ingredient of a bound on the optimal intervention cost.

</details>


### [109] [Gauss-Newton drifting extends natural gradient descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17167)
*Théo Dumont,Théo Lacombe,François-Xavier Vialard*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文分析 Drifting 模型的优化过程，揭示 Gauss-Newton 方案等价于参数空间中的自然梯度下降，从而在凸性假设下提供全局收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Drifting 方法作为生成模型训练的新范式备受关注，但其经验成功背后的优化机制尚不清楚，尤其在使用 Wasserstein 梯度作为 drift field 时，普通欧氏梯度下降难以利用凸性、无法获得收敛保证。

Method: 保持 Wasserstein 梯度作为 drift field，将梯度下降替换为 Gauss-Newton 方案，并分析该方案与参数空间中自然梯度下降的等价性。

Result: 证明了在 Wasserstein 梯度作为 drift field 下，Gauss-Newton 优化等价于参数空间的自然梯度下降。

Conclusion: 该等价关系使得在适当的凸性假设下，可以为优化过程提供全局收敛保证，从而解释 Drifting 方法的经验成功。

Abstract: Drifting methods have recently been introduced as a promising new paradigm for training generative models, and have attracted a lot of attention so far. In this work, we focus on how the optimization procedure shapes their convergence properties, shedding light on the underlying machinery that may explain their empirical success. More specifically, when the drift field is a Wasserstein gradient, optimizing the drifting loss via plain Euclidean gradient descent seems to forfeit any hope of exploiting convexity and therefore of obtaining any convergence guarantees. Changing either the drift field or the optimization procedure, however, may lead to different dynamics: we show that keeping the Wasserstein gradient as drift field but replacing gradient descent with a Gauss-Newton scheme amounts to performing a natural gradient descent in parameter space. This connection allows us to provide global convergence guarantees for the optimization procedure under suitable convexity assumptions.

</details>


### [110] [Pontryagin maximum principle for non-smooth state-constrained control problems over Wasserstein spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17197)
*Fernanda Urrea,Ernesto Treumún*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为Wasserstein空间上非局部连续性方程驱动的状态约束Bolza最优控制问题建立了Pontryagin最大值原理，允许代价泛函与约束仅局部Lipschitz且初始测度非紧支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Pontryagin最大值原理通常要求目标泛函与约束具有较强正则性（如全局Lipschitz或可微），且常假设初始测度紧支撑；本文希望放宽这些限制，处理仅局部Lipschitz、非紧初始测度的一般情形。

Method: 在Wasserstein空间上引入Clarke型确定性次微分；将Lasry-Lions正则化适配到Wasserstein空间，并结合Clarke次微分的稳定性结果以恢复一阶信息，进而证明最大值原理。

Result: 得到了局部Lipschitz、非紧支撑条件下的状态约束Bolza问题Pontryagin最大值原理；并证明对沿plan凸的泛函，该次微分具有全局支撑刻画，可给出全局极小点的一阶刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作推广了Wasserstein空间中Pontryagin最大值原理的适用范围，并提供了基于Clarke次微分的全局最优性刻画工具。

Abstract: We study a state-constrained Bolza optimal control problem governed by a non-local continuity equation in the Wasserstein space $\sP_2(\R^d)$. We develop a Pontryagin maximum principle for these problems in which both the cost functionals and the constraints are only locally Lipschitz, and the initial measure does not necessarily have compact support. To derive the main result, we introduce a notion of a Clarke-type deterministic subdifferential over $\sP_2(\R^d)$. The proof of the main result combines a careful adaptation of the Lasry-Lions regularization to the Wasserstein space coupled with stability results of the Clarke subdifferential that allow us to recover the first-order information. We also show that, for functionals convex along plans, this subdifferential admits a global supporting characterization and provides a first-order characterization of global minimizers.

</details>


### [111] [Data-Driven Stress Testing of Intermodal Freight Networks Using GAN-Generated Disruption Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17373)
*Xudong Wang,Mustafa Can Camur,Sabarna Choudhuri,Xueping Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合GAN与多式联运优化模型的压力测试框架，用于评估多式联运货运网络在复合中断下的韧性，发现GAN生成情景导致的成本远高于历史情景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多式联运货运网络面临日益增多的相关、多模式中断，但现有韧性评估多依赖历史或不相关情景，低估了系统性风险。

Method: 构建数据驱动的压力测试框架，将生成对抗网络（GAN）与多式联运优化模型相结合，以GAN生成的情景作为模拟输入来求解路径优化问题，获取系统成本并聚合结果以估计期望成本、识别主要风险驱动因素。案例研究聚焦田纳西河谷走廊的天气相关中断。

Result: 历史中断使总成本增加约3%，而GAN生成情景使成本增加超过25%，预期年成本达511万美元。风险集中在相关的多节点失效及关键节点（如诺克斯维尔港）。

Conclusion: 该框架有助于识别网络脆弱性并优先安排韧性投资。

Abstract: Intermodal freight networks are increasingly exposed to correlated, multi-mode disruptions, yet resilience assessments often rely on historical or uncorrelated scenarios that understate systemic risk. This paper develops a data-driven stress-testing framework integrating generative adversarial networks (GANs) with an intermodal optimization model to evaluate performance under realistic compound disruptions. The case study examines weather-related disruptions in the Tennessee Valley corridor. Each GAN-generated scenario is used as a simulation input, and the resulting routing problem is solved to obtain system costs. Aggregating outcomes enables estimation of expected costs and identification of major risk drivers. Results show that historical disruptions increase total cost by about 3%, whereas GAN-generated scenarios raise costs by over 25%, producing an expected annual cost of $5.11 million. Risk is concentrated in correlated multi-node failures and critical nodes such as the Port of Knoxville. The framework helps identify vulnerabilities and prioritize resilience investments.

</details>


### [112] [Subspace methods for min-max problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17375)
*Morteza Kimiaei,Shima Shabani,Michael Breuß*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出四类无雅可比子空间方法求解非线性单调方程，结合固定/变步长，给出全局收敛、残差速率、局部R-线性收敛及复杂度界，并在min-max问题上验证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大规模机器学习问题中的非线性单调方程，需要无雅可比、可扩展且收敛性有理论保证的子空间方法。

Method: 采用共轭梯度型的无雅可比子空间方向，配合固定步长或投影法变步长；通过角度条件与显式缩放控制搜索方向，确保收敛与子空间方向具体形式无关。

Result: 在单调性与Lipschitz连续假设下证明全局收敛及最优迭代残差速率O(ℓ^{-1/2})；局部误差界下距离解集以o(ℓ^{-1/2})衰减；可微且Jacobian非奇异时实现R-线性局部收敛与末迭代速率o(ℓ^{-1})；复杂度为O(ε^{-2})（基线）和O(log(ε^{-1}))（局部线性），并给出回溯残差评估一致界；还导出OGDA型残差-记忆表示。

Conclusion: 数值实验表明方法在代表性min-max问题上具有鲁棒性与高效性；理论涵盖全局与局部收敛、复杂度及与经典更新方向的关系。

Abstract: This paper introduces four groups of subspace methods for nonlinear monotone equations, with applications to large-scale machine learning problems. The methods use Jacobian-free subspace ({\tt JFS}) directions of conjugate-gradient type, combined with either fixed step sizes or variable step sizes generated by the projected method of Solodov and Svaiter. To ensure convergence independently of the specific algebraic form of the subspace directions, we impose an angle condition together with an explicit scaling rule controlling the effective search directions. Under monotonicity and Lipschitz continuity of the operator, we establish global convergence for both the line-search and fixed-step frameworks, as well as a best-iterate residual rate $O(\ell^{-1/2})$. Under a local error bound, the distance to the solution set satisfies the sharper decay $o(\ell^{-1/2})$. If the operator is continuously differentiable and its Jacobian is nonsingular at a solution, the required local error bound and local isolation follow, yielding $R$-linear local convergence. The residual sequence then converges geometrically and hence satisfies the last-iterate rate $o(\ell^{-1})$, without strong monotonicity. We also derive iteration and residual-evaluation complexity bounds: $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ for the baseline best-iterate guarantee and $O(\log(\varepsilon^{-1}))$ in the local linear regime, together with a uniform bound on backtracking residual evaluations. Under additional asymptotic assumptions, the proposed {\tt JFS} directions and several classical update directions admit related optimistic gradient descent--ascent (\texttt{OGDA})-type residual--memory representations. Numerical experiments illustrate the robustness and efficiency of the methods on representative min--max problems.

</details>


### [113] [Bridging the Gap Between Homogeneous and Heterogeneous Asynchronous Optimization Is Surprisingly Difficult](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17483)
*Alexander Tyurin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究异构分布式机器学习中最优时间复杂度能否被突破，证明在常用相似性假设、弱插值假设下不可能改进，并提出强插值+局部PL条件的新假设组合获得与同构最优匹配的复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模机器学习需多worker并行异步计算随机梯度。理论结果中同构（数据分布相同）与异构（各worker数据分布不同）两种设定存在显著时间复杂度差距，异构情形保证远为悲观。本文探究在何种假设下能克服这种悲观最优复杂度。

Method: 首先分析广泛使用的一阶和二阶相似性假设下任意随机算法的下界可达性；随后分析插值范式中的弱插值假设；最后引入强插值与局部Polyak-Lojasiewicz条件的最小组合，推导新的时间复杂度上界。

Result: 证明在一阶/二阶相似性假设下，任意随机算法都无法改进悲观最优复杂度；弱插值假设同样不足；强插值+局部PL条件可得到与同构设定最优结果中worker计算时间依赖相同的复杂度，且无需相同数据分布。

Conclusion: 异构设定的悲观复杂度在常用假设下本质上不可改进；只有结合强插值与局部PL这类不可约假设，才能在异构条件下达到同构最优时间复杂度。

Abstract: Modern large-scale machine learning tasks often require multiple workers, devices, CPUs, or GPUs to compute stochastic gradients in parallel and asynchronously to train model weights. Theoretical results typically distinguish between two settings: (i) the homogeneous setting, where all workers have access to the same data distribution, and (ii) the heterogeneous setting, where each worker operates on different data distributions. Known optimal time complexities in these settings reveal a significant gap, with far more pessimistic guarantees in the heterogeneous case. In this work, we investigate whether these pessimistic optimal time complexities can be overcome under different assumptions. Surprisingly, we show that improvement is provably impossible under widely used first- and second-order similarity assumptions for any randomized algorithm. We then turn to the interpolation regime and demonstrate that the weak interpolation assumption alone is also insufficient. Finally, we introduce a minimal combination of irreducible assumptions, strong interpolation and the local Polyak-Lojasiewicz condition, to derive a new time complexity bound that matches the dependence on worker computation times in the best-known result in the homogeneous setting, without requiring identical data distributions.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Comparative Analysis on Inertia Estimation Algorithms (IEAs) in Providing Proper Frequency Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16254)
*Karl M. H. Lai,Yunhe Hou,Kwunhang Wong*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文对电力系统惯量估计算法进行系统性比较分析，涵盖滤波、拟合、RLS、Kalman滤波等方法，并探讨其对风电惯量模拟策略的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 惯量常数H是电力系统韧性的核心指标，影响频率备用调度、需求响应、系统解耦与控制。传统仅按同步发电机动能定义，忽略了HVDC与IBR中非线性动态及控制可释放的功率，且噪声测量、事件驱动参数跳变和位置暂态响应使实时惯量估计困难。

Method: 系统性对比分析惯量估计算法（IEAs）：将测量法中的滤波与拟合（经典方法）与数据驱动的递归最小二乘（RLS）、基于模型的Kalman滤波（KF）进行基准比较。

Result: 提供了模型驱动与数据驱动惯量估计方法的整体综述，并探索了IEAs对风电惯量模拟策略的影响。

Conclusion: 未来低惯量电网安全运行需要鲁棒、自适应、数据驱动的惯量估计框架。

Abstract: The inertia constant H[s] is a fundamental indicator of power system resilience, linking power imbalance between generation and load to frequency deviation. It is essential in frequency reserve dispatch under stability-constrained optimal power flow (OPF), demand response (DR) in ancillary service, system decoupling and frequency control in modern power system. While the inertia constant is traditionally defined as the intrinsic kinetic energy of synchronous generators on bar normalized to the power base, this neglects the releasable power under nonlinear dynamics and control inside HVDC and Inverter-based Resources (IBRs). Accurate real-time inertia estimation is therefore essential to perform proper frequency control and to indicate the risks of failure in frequency restoration. It, however, is challenging with noisy frequency measurement under event-driven parameter jumps and locational transient responses. This paper presents a systematic comparative analysis on inertia estimation algorithms (IEAs) for frequency response applications. Classical methods such as filtering and fitting under measurement-based methods are benchmarked against data-based parameter estimation techniques such as recursive least squares (RLS) and model-based methods such as Kalman filtering (KF). The main contributions are: (i) a holistic review of model- and data-based inertia estimation methods, (ii) exploration on the effect of IEA to wind-based inertia emulation strategies. The findings underscore the need for robust, adaptive, and data-driven estimation frameworks to ensure secure operation of future low-inertia grids.

</details>


### [115] [An Integrated EMT Small-Signal Stability Analysis Tool for Power Systems with High Inverter-Based Resource Penetration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16273)
*Zihao Qin,Xiaonan Lu,Shuan Dong,Jin Tan*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出EMT-SSA工具套件，实现IBR富集电力系统的电磁暂态级小信号稳定性分析，可直接从PSS/E快照生成特征值、阻尼比和参与因子等模态指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步机组被逆变器型资源(IBR)取代，引入超出传统机电频段的快速换流器控制动态。传统相量域小信号工具无法解析次同步与换流器驱动动态，而EMT仿真器只能输出时域波形，缺乏稳定性评估所需的模态指标。

Method: EMT-SSA从标准PSS/E系统快照(.raw/.dyr)出发，将模型转换为高保真EMT表示；求解扩展潮流以获得网络与设备控制器的稳态平衡点；对全阶EMT模型线性化构建状态矩阵，进而计算特征值、振荡模态与参与因子。设备库涵盖同步发电机、跟网型与构网型逆变器、输电线路和负荷。

Result: 该工具套件能够识别阻尼不足或不稳定的模态，并将其归因到具体设备。在Kundur两区域系统上进行了验证，模态结果与PSS/E NEVA对标。

Conclusion: EMT-SSA填补了EMT建模与模态稳定性分析之间的空白，为IBR富集系统提供了一套集成的EMT级小信号分析工具，并可识别和定位稳定性风险设备。

Abstract: The ongoing replacement of synchronous generation by inverter-based resources (IBRs) introduces fast converter control dynamics whose characteristic frequencies extend beyond the classical electromechanical band. Conventional small-signal stability tools are commonly formulated in the phasor domain, representing electrical quantities as slowly varying phasors at the fundamental frequency, and therefore cannot resolve the sub-synchronous and converter-driven dynamics that increasingly arise in operation. Electromagnetic Transient (EMT) modeling captures these dynamics, but established EMT simulators produce time-domain waveforms rather than the modal indicators (eigenvalues, damping ratios, and participation factors) needed to assess stability risk. This paper presents EMT-SSA, an integrated tool suite for EMT-level small-signal analysis of IBR-rich power systems. From a standard PSS/E system snapshot (.raw/.dyr), it converts the model into an EMT representation with enhanced fidelity, solves an extended power flow for the steady-state equilibrium points of both the network and the device controllers, and linearizes a full-order EMT model to form the system state matrix, from which it produces eigenvalues, oscillation modes, and participation factor analysis results over a device library spanning synchronous generators, grid-following and grid-forming inverters, transmission lines, and loads. The EMT-SSA tool suite identifies poorly damped or unstable modes and attributes them to specific devices. The tool is demonstrated on the Kundur two-area system, with its modal results benchmarked against PSS/E NEVA.

</details>


### [116] [Policy Gradient over History-Dependent Policy Classes for LQR with Domain Randomization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16300)
*Tesshu Fujinami,Bruce D. Lee,Anastasios Tsiamis,Nikolai Matni,George J. Pappas*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究域随机化LQR下基于策略梯度训练历史依赖控制器（如FIR控制器）的优化景观，提出基于课程学习的算法逐步扩展控制器记忆，证明在环境异质性有界时PG全局收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域随机化(DR)广泛用于通过强化学习在模拟环境分布上训练控制器以克服sim-to-real差距，但即使在线性二次调节器(LQR)目标下，策略梯度(PG)的优化景观仍缺乏理解。

Method: 首先研究域随机化LQR在历史依赖策略类（如有限脉冲响应FIR控制器）上的策略梯度，这类控制器可扩展同时稳定的可能性；随后提出一种基于课程学习的算法，逐步扩展控制器的记忆长度以找到稳定控制器。

Result: 证明在环境异质性满足适当界的条件下，PG结合所提算法能够全局收敛到DR目标样本平均近似的最小值点；实验结果支持理论发现。

Conclusion: 研究为域随机化控制的优化理论提供新见解，并为未来工作指明有希望的方向，包括非线性域随机化控制。

Abstract: Domain Randomization (DR) has been widely used to overcome the sim-to-real gap by training a controller on a distribution of simulated environments via reinforcement learning. While DR can achieve robust performance simply using controllers synthesized via policy gradient (PG) methods, the optimization landscape is not well understood, even in the case of linear quadratic regulator (LQR) objectives. To this end, we first study PG of domain randomized LQR over history-dependent policy classes, such as finite impulse response controllers, as they can extend the possibilities of simultaneous stabilization. Second, to find such a stabilizing controller, we propose a curriculum learning based algorithm which gradually expands the memory of the controller. Finally, we show that PG with the proposed algorithm converges globally to the minimizer of a sample average approximation of the DR objective under suitable bounds on the heterogeneity of environments.
